\displaystyle=-\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}{v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}.

(82)

The evaluation of the third term follows in the same fashion,

\displaystyle\braket{\widehat{VP}_{3}}

\displaystyle=-\int\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_{i}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_{j}\,{v}_{ij}\rho_{A}(\mathbf{x}_{i}|\mathbf{x}_{l})\Gamma_{B}(\mathbf{x}_{j},\mathbf{x}_{l}|\mathbf{x}_{j},\mathbf{x}_{i})

(83)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}\int\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_{i}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_{j}{v}_{ij}D^{A}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}d^{B}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}\phi^{*}_{\textsc{p}_{1}}(\mathbf{x}_{i})\phi_{\textsc{p}_{2}}(\mathbf{x}_{l})\phi_{\textsc{q}_{1}}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{j})\phi_{\textsc{q}_{2}}(\mathbf{x}_{j})\phi^{*}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}(\mathbf{x}_{l})\phi_{\textsc{q}_{4}}(\mathbf{x}_{i})

(84)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}S^{\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{p}_{2}}{v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}D^{A}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}d^{B}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}

(85)

The effective operator is written as:

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{3}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}S^{\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{p}_{2}}{v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{a}_{\textsc{p}_{1}}^{\dagger}\hat{a}_{\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{b}^{\dagger}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}\hat{b}^{\dagger}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}\hat{b}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}\hat{b}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}

(86)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}S^{\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{p}_{2}}{v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}.

(87)

Finally, the evaluation of the last term is given by:

\displaystyle\braket{\widehat{VP}_{4}}

\displaystyle=-\int\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_{k}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_{i}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_{j}\,{v}_{ij}\Gamma_{A}(\mathbf{x}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{k}|\mathbf{x}_{i}\mathbf{x}_{l})\Gamma_{B}(\mathbf{x}_{j},\mathbf{x}_{l}|\mathbf{x}_{j}\mathbf{x}_{k})

(88)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}\int\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_{k}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_{l}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_{i}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{x}_{j}{v}_{ij}d^{A}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}d^{B}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}\times

(89)

\displaystyle\phi_{\textsc{p}_{1}}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{i})\phi_{\textsc{p}_{2}}(\mathbf{x}_{i})\phi^{*}_{\textsc{p}_{3}}(\mathbf{x}_{k})\phi_{\textsc{p}_{4}}(\mathbf{x}_{l})\phi_{\textsc{q}_{1}}^{*}(\mathbf{x}_{j})\phi_{\textsc{q}_{2}}(\mathbf{x}_{j})\phi^{*}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}(\mathbf{x}_{l})\phi_{\textsc{q}_{4}}(\mathbf{x}_{k})

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}S^{\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{p}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}{v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}\;d^{A}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}d^{B}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}

(90)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}S^{\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{p}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}{v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}d^{A}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}d^{B}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}

(91)

resulting in the operator,

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{4}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}{v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{p}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}\hat{a}^{\dagger}_{\textsc{p}_{1}}\hat{a}^{\dagger}_{\textsc{p}_{3}}\hat{a}_{\textsc{p}_{4}}\hat{a}_{\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{b}^{\dagger}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}\hat{b}^{\dagger}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}\hat{b}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}\hat{b}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}

(92)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}{v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{p}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}\,\hat{e}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}.

(93)

Combining all of the results from above, we find the total electrostatic-exchange operator,

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}\end{subarray}}v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}{v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{4}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}S^{\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{p}_{2}}{v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}{v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{p}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}.

(94)

The contribution of the second term,
P_{c}V_{c}
, in Eq. (
69
) will be given by:

\displaystyle\widehat{PV}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}\end{subarray}}v^{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}S^{\textsc{q}_{1}}_{\textsc{p}_{4}}{v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{p_{3}\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}{v}^{\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}{v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{p}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}.

(95)

Resulting in the total electrostatic-exchange operator given by:

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{\text{s}}=

\displaystyle\;\;\;\;\tfrac{1}{2}(\widehat{VP}+\widehat{PV})

(96)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}\left(v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}+v^{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}\left({v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}+{v}^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{p}_{4}\textsc{q}_{1}}S_{\textsc{p}_{3}}^{\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{4}\textsc{p}_{3}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}\left({v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{p}_{2}}\,\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}+{v}^{\textsc{q}_{4}\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}S_{\textsc{q}_{3}}^{\textsc{p}_{2}}\,\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}\textsc{q}_{3}}\right)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}\left({v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{p}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}+{v}^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}S_{\textsc{q}_{3}}^{\textsc{p}_{4}}S_{\textsc{p}_{3}}^{\textsc{q}_{4}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{4}\textsc{p}_{3}}\hat{e}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}\textsc{q}_{3}}\right).

(97)

This recovers the electrostatic-exchange operator defined in the main text. Note that compared to the electrostatic and exchange operators,
\hat{V}
and
\hat{P}
, the electrostatic-exchange operator contains products of high-order tensors. It is possible to gain more insight by rewriting this operator in the so-called chemist notation where single-excitation and spin-summed operators are combined,

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{\text{s}}=

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}\left(\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}+\nu^{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}\left(\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}+\nu^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{p}_{4}\textsc{q}_{1}}S_{\textsc{p}_{3}}^{\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{4}\textsc{p}_{3}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}\right)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}\left(\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{p}_{2}}\,\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}+\nu^{\textsc{q}_{4}\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}S_{\textsc{q}_{3}}^{\textsc{p}_{2}}\,\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{4}\textsc{q}_{3}}\right)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}\left(v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{p}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}+v^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}S_{\textsc{q}_{3}}^{\textsc{p}_{4}}S_{\textsc{p}_{3}}^{\textsc{q}_{4}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{4}\textsc{p}_{3}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{4}\textsc{q}_{3}}\right),

(98)

where the renormalized tensor coefficients used in the chemist notation are defined as:

\displaystyle\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}

\displaystyle=v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}-\sum_{\textsc{q}_{3}}S^{\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{p}_{2}}{v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}}-\sum_{\textsc{p}_{3}}v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}+\sum_{\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{q}_{3}}v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}},

(99)

\displaystyle\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{4}}

\displaystyle=v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{4}}-\sum_{\textsc{q}_{3}}v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}},

(100)

\displaystyle\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}

\displaystyle=v_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}}-\sum_{\textsc{p}_{3}}v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}.

(101)

The renormalized tensor coefficients for the complex conjugate terms are also defined appropriately. In summary, these equations recover the final expressions in the main paper using the spin-orbital notation. In this form, it is possible to see that the first term in the fourth line of Eq. (
98
) exactly equals the product of the electrostatic and exchange operators,
\hat{V}\hat{P}
. Using the symmetry,
\hat{e}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}=\hat{e}_{\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}
and
\hat{e}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}=\hat{e}_{\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}
, it is possible to see that the second term equals the product
\hat{P}\hat{V}
. The first three lines are now more clearly contributions that arise from the truncated basis set. In Appendix B, we show how these terms cancel exactly in the complete basis set limit.

B.3
SAPT operator symmetries

In the following, we discuss the permutational symmetries inherent to the SAPT operators defined above. For complex orbitals, the four-index intermolecular tensor obeys the Hermitian symmetry,

v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}=[v^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}]^{*},

(102)

while the intermolecular overlap matrix obeys,

S^{\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}}=[S^{\textsc{q}}_{\textsc{p}}]^{*}.

(103)

For real orbitals, the intermolecular tensor obeys the four-fold symmetry,

v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}=v^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}=v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}=v^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}},

(104)

while the intermolecular overlap matrix symmetry becomes,

S^{\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}}=S^{\textsc{q}}_{\textsc{p}}.

(105)

It is important to note that while the two-electron integral,
(\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}|\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2})=\int\mathrm{d}\mathbf{r}_{i}\mathrm{d}\mathbf{r}_{j}\;\phi_{\textsc{p}_{1}}^{*}(\mathbf{r}_{i})\phi_{\textsc{p}_{2}}(\mathbf{r}_{i})r_{ij}^{-1}\phi_{\textsc{q}_{1}}^{*}(\mathbf{r}_{j})\phi_{\textsc{q}_{2}}(\mathbf{r}_{j})
, obeys the symmetry,
(\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}|\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2})=(\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}|\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2})
, the full intermolecular tensor in Eq. (
49
) does not obey this type of symmetry. The intermolecular tensor only has a four-fold symmetry as opposed to the typical eight-fold symmetry encountered in the conventional quantum chemistry Hamiltonian. These considerations will be important for the development of the SAPT-EVE algorithm and the block encoding methodologies encountered shortly. For the rest of the appendices as well as the main manuscript, we will assume real orbitals for all of the calculations and resource estimates. As a result, we will also assume that the prepared ground-state wavefunctions will be fully real resulting in the following permutational symmetries for the reduced density matrix elements of monomer A:

\displaystyle D^{A}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}

\displaystyle=D^{A}_{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}},

(106)

\displaystyle d^{A}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}

\displaystyle=d^{A}_{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{4}\textsc{p}_{3}}=d^{A}_{\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}=d^{A}_{\textsc{p}_{4}\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}},

(107)

as well as monomer B:

\displaystyle D^{B}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}

\displaystyle=D^{B}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}},

(108)

\displaystyle d^{B}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}

\displaystyle=d^{B}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}\textsc{q}_{3}}=d^{B}_{\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}=d^{B}_{\textsc{q}_{4}\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}.

(109)

Taking into account all of these symmetries, we re-write all of the SAPT operators as:

\displaystyle\hat{V}=

\displaystyle\;\;\;\sum_{\textbf{{p}},\textbf{{q}}}\text{sym}(v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}})\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}\,,

(110)

\displaystyle\hat{P}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{{p}},\textbf{{q}}\end{subarray}}\text{sym}(S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}})\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}},\,

(111)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{\mathrm{s}}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{{p}},\textbf{{q}}\end{subarray}}\text{sym}(\bar{\nu}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}})\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}

(112)

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{{p}},\textbf{{q}}\end{subarray}}\text{sym}(\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}})\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{{p}},\textbf{{q}}\end{subarray}}\text{sym}(\nu_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}})\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{{p}},\textbf{{q}}\end{subarray}}\text{sym}(v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}})\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}\,,

where

\displaystyle\bar{\nu}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}

\displaystyle=v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\Big[\sum_{\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{q}_{3}}(v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}+v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}})-\sum_{\textsc{q}_{3}}(v_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{3}}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}+v_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{3}}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}})-\sum_{\textsc{p}_{3}}(v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}+v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}})\Big]

(113)

\displaystyle\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{4}}

\displaystyle=v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{4}}-\sum_{\textsc{q}_{3}}v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}\,,

(114)

\displaystyle\nu_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}}

\displaystyle=v_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}}-\sum_{\textsc{p}_{3}}v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}},

(115)

where we emphasize that
\bar{\nu}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}
is different from
\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}
in the main text. The operator,
\text{sym}(\cdot)
, symmetrizes all of the tensors with respect to the monomer indices
\textsc{p}/\textsc{q}
independently using the permutational symmetries associated with the 1- and 2- RDMS in Eqs. (
106
)-(
109
). The permutational-invariant SAPT tensors are written explicitly as:

\displaystyle\text{sym}(S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{{\textsc{q}_{2}}}S^{{\textsc{p}_{2}}}_{{\textsc{q}_{1}}})

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{4}(S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{{\textsc{q}_{2}}}S^{{\textsc{p}_{2}}}_{{\textsc{q}_{1}}}+S^{{\textsc{p}_{2}}}_{{\textsc{q}_{2}}}S^{{\textsc{p}_{1}}}_{{\textsc{q}_{1}}}+S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{{\textsc{q}_{1}}}S^{{\textsc{p}_{2}}}_{{\textsc{q}_{2}}}+S^{{\textsc{p}_{2}}}_{{\textsc{q}_{1}}}S^{{\textsc{p}_{1}}}_{{\textsc{q}_{2}}})=\tfrac{1}{2}(S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{{\textsc{q}_{2}}}S^{{\textsc{p}_{2}}}_{{\textsc{q}_{1}}}+S^{{\textsc{p}_{2}}}_{{\textsc{q}_{2}}}S^{{\textsc{p}_{1}}}_{{\textsc{q}_{1}}}),

(116)

\displaystyle\text{sym}(\nu^{{\textsc{p}_{1}}{\textsc{p}_{2}}}_{{\textsc{q}_{1}}{\textsc{q}_{2}}})

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{4}(\nu^{{\textsc{p}_{1}}{\textsc{p}_{2}}}_{{\textsc{q}_{1}}{\textsc{q}_{2}}}+\nu^{{\textsc{p}_{2}}{\textsc{p}_{1}}}_{{\textsc{q}_{1}}{\textsc{q}_{2}}}+\nu^{{\textsc{p}_{1}}{\textsc{p}_{2}}}_{{\textsc{q}_{2}}{\textsc{q}_{1}}}+\nu^{{\textsc{p}_{2}}{\textsc{p}_{1}}}_{{\textsc{q}_{2}}{\textsc{q}_{1}}}),

(117)

\displaystyle\text{sym}(\bar{\nu}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}})

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{4}(\bar{\nu}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}+\bar{\nu}^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{1}}+\bar{\nu}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{p}_{2}}+\bar{\nu}^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}),

(118)

\displaystyle\text{sym}(\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}})

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{8}(\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}+\nu^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}+\nu^{\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}+\nu^{\textsc{p}_{4}\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{1}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}

(119)

\displaystyle+\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{p}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}+\nu^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{p}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}+\nu^{\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{p}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}+\nu^{\textsc{p}_{4}\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}),

\displaystyle\text{sym}(\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}})

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{8}(\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}+\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}+\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}+\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}\textsc{q}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}

(120)

\displaystyle+\nu^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{q}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}+\nu^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}+\nu^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}+\nu^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}\textsc{q}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}),

\displaystyle\text{sym}(v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}})

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{16}\Big[v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}+v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}+v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}S^{\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}+v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}\textsc{q}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}

(121)

\displaystyle\;\;\;\;\;\;+v^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}+v^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}S^{\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}+v^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}+v^{\textsc{p}_{2}\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}\textsc{q}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}

\displaystyle\;\;\;\;\;\;+v^{\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}+v^{\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}+v^{\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}+v^{\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}\textsc{q}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}

\displaystyle\;\;\;\;\;\;+v^{\textsc{p}_{4}\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}+v^{\textsc{p}_{4}\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}\textsc{q}_{1}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}}+v^{\textsc{p}_{4}\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}+v^{\textsc{p}_{4}\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}\textsc{q}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}\Big].

B.4
Spatial orbital basis

The intermolecular interaction and overlap tensors in the spin-orbital basis are related to the corresponding spatial orbital tensors through the expressions,

\displaystyle{v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}

\displaystyle={v}^{p_{1}p_{2}}_{q_{1}q_{2}}\delta_{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}\delta_{\tau_{1}\tau_{2}},

(122)

and

S^{\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}}=S^{p}_{q}\delta_{\sigma\tau}.

(123)

The spatial orbital basis reduces the memory storage requirements on the classical computer while also speeding up the factorization procedures (i.e. eigendecomposition and/or singular value decomposition) required for the tensor factorization techniques discussed in the main manuscript. As we show below, the spatial orbital basis also helps reduce the total number of terms required for the data-loading oracle on the quantum computer by exploiting the spin-projection symmetries for each of the two monomers defined as:

\displaystyle\hat{S}_{z}^{(A)}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{p}(\hat{a}^{\dagger}_{p\alpha}\hat{a}_{p\alpha}-\hat{a}^{\dagger}_{p\beta}\hat{a}_{p\beta}),

(124)

and

\displaystyle\hat{S}_{z}^{(B)}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{q}(\hat{b}^{\dagger}_{q\alpha}\hat{b}_{q\alpha}-\hat{b}^{\dagger}_{q\beta}\hat{b}_{q\beta}).

(125)

Converting into the spatial orbital basis and taking into account the spin-projection symmetries for each monomer, we find that the electrostatic and exchange operators may be written as:

\displaystyle\hat{V}=

\displaystyle\;\;\;\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{p}\mathbf{q}\\
\sigma\tau\end{subarray}}\text{sym}(v^{p_{1}p_{2}}_{q_{1}q_{2}})\hat{E}_{p_{1}p_{2}}^{\sigma}\hat{E}_{q_{1}q_{2}}^{\tau}\,,

(126)

\displaystyle\hat{P}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{p}\textbf{q}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}\text{sym}(S^{p_{1}}_{q_{2}}S^{p_{2}}_{q_{1}})\hat{E}_{p_{1}p_{2}}^{\sigma}\hat{E}_{q_{1}q_{2}}^{\sigma},

(127)

while the electrostatic-exchange operator arising from
V_{c}P_{c}
in Eq. (
94
) is written as:

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{p}\textbf{q}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}\left({\nu}^{p_{1}q_{2}}_{q_{1}p_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{p_{1}p_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{q_{1}q_{2}}+\nu^{p_{1}p_{2}}_{q_{1}p_{4}}S^{p_{3}}_{q_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{p_{1}p_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{p_{3}p_{4}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{q_{1}q_{2}}+\nu_{q_{1}q_{2}}^{p_{1}q_{4}}S^{p_{2}}_{q_{3}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{p_{1}p_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{q_{1}q_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{q_{3}q_{4}}\right)+\hat{V}\hat{P}.

(128)

Appendix C
Complete basis set limit

In the following, we show that the equality,
\widehat{VP}=\hat{V}\hat{P}
, holds in the complete basis limit for the electrostatic-exchange operator defined in the spatial orbital basis defined by Eq. (
128
). The proof is based on the identity,

\delta(\mathbf{r}-\mathbf{r}^{\prime})=\sum_{n}\phi^{*}_{n}(\mathbf{r})\phi_{n}(\mathbf{r}^{\prime}),

(129)

which only holds for complete basis sets. This identity leads to the following formula in the spatial orbital basis,

\displaystyle\sum_{p_{3}}S^{p_{3}}_{q_{4}}v^{p_{1}p_{3}}_{q_{1}q_{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{p_{3}}\iint\!d\mathbf{r}d\mathbf{r}_{i}d\mathbf{r}_{j}\,\phi^{*}_{p_{3}}(\mathbf{r})\phi_{q_{4}}(\mathbf{r})\phi^{*}_{p_{1}}(\mathbf{r}_{i})\phi_{p_{3}}(\mathbf{r}_{i})v_{ij}\phi^{*}_{q_{1}}(\mathbf{r}_{j})\phi_{q_{2}}(\mathbf{r}_{j})

(130)

\displaystyle=\iint\!d\mathbf{r}d\mathbf{r}_{i}d\mathbf{r}_{j}\,\delta(\mathbf{r}-\mathbf{r}_{i})\phi_{q_{4}}(\mathbf{r})\phi^{*}_{p_{1}}(\mathbf{r}_{i})v_{ij}\phi^{*}_{q_{1}}(\mathbf{r}_{j})\phi_{q_{2}}(\mathbf{r}_{j})

(131)

\displaystyle=v^{p_{1}q_{4}}_{q_{1}q_{2}},

(132)

which is used throughout the calculation. Starting with the third term of Eq. (
128
), we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{p}\textbf{q}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}\nu_{q_{1}q_{2}}^{p_{1}q_{4}}S_{p_{2}}^{q_{3}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{p_{1}p_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{q_{1}q_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{q_{3}q_{4}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{p}\textbf{q}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}\Big(v_{q_{1}q_{2}}^{p_{1}q_{4}}S_{p_{2}}^{q_{3}}-\sum_{p}v^{p_{1}p}_{q_{1}q_{2}}S^{p}_{q_{4}}S_{p_{2}}^{q_{3}}\Big)E_{p_{1}p_{2}}^{\sigma}\hat{E}^{+}_{q_{1}q_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{q_{3}q_{4}}

(133)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{p}\textbf{q}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}\Big(v_{q_{1}q_{2}}^{p_{1}q_{4}}S_{p_{2}}^{q_{3}}-v^{p_{1}q_{4}}_{q_{1}q_{2}}S_{p_{2}}^{q_{3}}\Big)E_{p_{1}p_{2}}^{\sigma}\hat{E}^{+}_{q_{1}q_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{q_{3}q_{4}}

(134)

\displaystyle=0.

(135)

The second term in Eq. (
128
) is evaluated as:

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{p}\textbf{q}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}\nu^{p_{1}p_{2}}_{q_{1}p_{4}}S^{p_{3}}_{q_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{p_{1}p_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{p_{3}p_{4}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{q_{1}q_{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{p}\textbf{q}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}\Big(v^{p_{1}p_{2}}_{q_{1}p_{4}}S^{p_{3}}_{q_{2}}-\sum_{q}v^{p_{1}p_{2}}_{q_{1}q}S^{p_{3}}_{q_{2}}S_{p_{4}}^{q}\Big)E_{p_{1}p_{2}}^{+}E_{p_{3}p_{4}}^{\sigma}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{q_{1}q_{2}}

(136)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{p}\textbf{q}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}\Big(v^{p_{1}p_{2}}_{q_{1}p_{4}}S^{p_{3}}_{q_{2}}-v^{p_{1}p_{2}}_{q_{1}p_{4}}S^{p_{3}}_{q_{2}}\Big)E_{p_{1}p_{2}}^{+}E_{p_{3}p_{4}}^{\sigma}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{q_{1}q_{2}}

(137)

\displaystyle=0,

(138)

while the first term is given by:

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{p}\textbf{q}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}{\nu}^{p_{1}q_{2}}_{q_{1}p_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{p_{1}p_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{q_{1}q_{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{p}\textbf{q}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}\Big(v^{p_{1}q_{2}}_{q_{1}p_{2}}-\sum_{q}v_{q_{1}q}^{p_{1}q_{2}}S^{q}_{p_{2}}-\sum_{p}v^{p_{1}p}_{q_{1}p_{2}}S^{p}_{q_{2}}+\sum_{pq}v^{p_{1}p}_{q_{1}q}S^{p}_{q_{2}}S_{p_{2}}^{q}\Big)E_{p_{1}p_{2}}^{\sigma}E_{q_{1}q_{2}}^{\sigma}

(139)

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{p}\textbf{q}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}\Big(v^{p_{1}q_{2}}_{q_{1}p_{2}}-v_{q_{1}p_{2}}^{p_{1}q_{2}}-v^{p_{1}q_{2}}_{q_{1}p_{2}}+v^{p_{1}q_{2}}_{q_{1}p_{2}}\Big)E_{p_{1}p_{2}}^{\sigma}E_{q_{1}q_{2}}^{\sigma}

(140)

\displaystyle=0.

(141)

As a result, we are only left with the fourth term such that,
\widehat{VP}=\hat{V}\hat{P}
. Ultimately, this explains the form of
\widehat{VP}
consisting of four terms compared the simpler
\hat{V}\hat{P}
term that one might have expected from the beginning. It is important to note that this equality does not hold exactly for the electrostatic-exchange operator defined by Eq. (
112
). Further studies of these properties and their relevance to the SAPT-EVE algorithm and the
\ell_{1}
norm is left for future work.

Appendix D
Active Space Formulation

In the following, we develop an active space formulation of SAPT where the orbitals are decomposed into three parts: (1) core orbitals which are assumed to be fully occupied, (2) active space orbitals where we expect the main physics/chemistry to occur, and (3) virtual orbitals which have higher energy than the active space orbitals but provide a negligible contribution to the ground-state energy. Active space methods are important for the description of large-scale systems. In the following, we use a notation where
i/t
and
j/u
indices correspond to the core/active orbitals of monomers A and B respectively. The active space decomposition is illustrated for the electrostatic (Sec.
D.1
), exchange (Sec.
D.2
), and electrostatic-exchange (Sec.
D.3
) operators below. Throughout this appendix, we will use Einstein notation exclusively with the core orbital indices (
i/j
) to reduce the verbosity of the equations. We will also ignore the symmetrization of the tensor elements but will discuss the symmetrized version of the active space expressions near the end. Real orbitals are also assumed throughout.

D.1
Electrostatics

We first consider the full-space electrostatic operator,

\displaystyle\hat{V}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}{v}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{E}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}\\
\sigma\tau\end{subarray}}{v}^{p_{1}p_{2}}_{q_{1}q_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{p_{1}p_{2}}\hat{E}^{\tau}_{q_{1}q_{2}}

(142)

The active space operator is derived by tracing out the inactive degrees of freedom with a Hartree-Fock-like wavefunction in the inactive space. As a result, we require decomposing the summation of each of the indices in terms of core
i/j
and active space
t/u
indices,

\displaystyle\sum_{p_{1}p_{2}\in A}\sum_{q_{1}q_{2}\in B}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i_{1}i_{2}\in A}\sum_{j_{1}j_{2}\in B}+\sum_{t_{1}t_{2}\in A}\sum_{j_{1}j_{2}\in B}+\sum_{i_{1}i_{2}\in A}\sum_{u_{1}u_{2}\in B}+\sum_{t_{1}t_{2}\in A}\sum_{u_{1}u_{2}\in B}.

(143)

The virtual orbitals are assumed to remain unoccupied therefore they will not contribute to the active space renormalization procedure. By tracing out the core orbitals, the active space electrostatic operator may be written as:

\displaystyle\hat{V}_{\mathrm{active}}

\displaystyle=v^{i_{1}i_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}D^{A}_{i_{1}i_{2}}D^{B}_{j_{1}j_{2}}+\sum_{t_{1}t_{2}\in A}\!\!v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{t_{1}t_{2}}D^{B}_{j_{1}j_{2}}+\sum_{u_{1}u_{2}\in B}\!\!v^{i_{1}i_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}D^{A}_{i_{1}i_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{u_{1}u_{2}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}t_{1}t_{2}\in A\\
u_{1}u_{2}\in B\end{subarray}}\!\!v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{u_{1}u_{2}}.

(144)

defined with respect to the core-orbital-based reduced density matrix,
D_{i_{1}i_{2}}^{(A)}
/
D_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{B}
, for monomers A/B. Assuming that the core orbitals are doubly occupied in a closed-shell configuration,
D^{X}_{nn^{\prime}}=2\delta_{nn^{\prime}}
, we obtain the following active space electrostatic operator:

\hat{V}_{\mathrm{active}}=4v^{ii}_{jj}+2\sum_{\mathbf{t}}v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{jj}\hat{E}^{+}_{t_{1}t_{2}}+2\sum_{\mathbf{u}}v^{ii}_{u_{1}u_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{u_{1}u_{2}}+\sum_{\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}}v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{u_{1}u_{2}},

(145)

Note that it is possible to combine all of the zero-body and one-body terms into a spin-independent four-index tensor:

\displaystyle\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}=v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}+2v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{jj}\delta_{u_{1}u_{2}}/\tilde{\eta}_{B}+2v^{ii}_{u_{1}u_{2}}\delta_{t_{1}t_{2}}/\tilde{\eta}_{A}+4v^{ii}_{jj}\delta_{t_{1}t_{2}}\delta_{u_{1}u_{2}}/(\tilde{\eta}_{A}\tilde{\eta}_{B}),

(146)

where
\tilde{\eta}_{A}
and
\tilde{\eta}_{B}
correspond to the total number of electrons in the active spaces of monomer A and B respectively. Using this formulation, we obtain the final form of the active-space electrostatic operator:

\hat{V}_{\mathrm{active}}=\sum_{\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}}\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{u_{1}u_{2}}.

(147)

D.2
Exchange

The full space exchange term is given by Eq. (
68
) which, following the same steps as above, results in the following expression for the active space exchange operator:

\hat{P}_{\mathrm{active}}=-2S^{i}_{j}S_{i}^{j}-\sum_{\mathbf{t}}S^{t_{1}}_{j}S_{t_{2}}^{j}\hat{E}^{+}_{t_{1}t_{2}}-\sum_{\mathbf{u}}S_{i}^{u_{1}}S^{i}_{u_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{u_{1}u_{2}}-\sum_{\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}}S_{u_{2}}^{t_{1}}S_{t_{2}}^{u_{1}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{u_{1}u_{2}}.

(148)

D.3
Electrostatic-Exchange

The derivation of the active space operators for the electrostatic-exchange term is much more involved. We proceed by deriving the active space operators for each of the four terms separately. For notational clarily, we also define

\hat{e}_{\mathbf{t}}^{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}=(\hat{E}^{\sigma_{1}}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{2}}_{t_{3}t_{4}}-\delta_{t_{2}t_{3}}^{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{2}}_{t_{1}t_{4}})
and
\hat{e}_{\mathbf{u}}^{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}=(\hat{E}^{\sigma_{1}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{2}}_{u_{3}u_{4}}-\delta_{u_{2}u_{3}}^{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{4}})
.

D.3.1
\widehat{VP}_{1}

The first term of the full space electrostatic-exchange operator is given by Eq. (
94
) which, following the same steps as above, results in the following expression for the active space operator:

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{\mathrm{1,active}}

\displaystyle=-2v^{ij}_{ji}-\sum_{\mathbf{u}}v^{iu_{2}}_{u_{1}i}\hat{E}_{u_{1}u_{2}}^{+}-\sum_{\mathbf{t}}v^{t_{1}j}_{jt_{2}}\hat{E}_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{+}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}v^{t_{1}u_{2}}_{u_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{u_{1}u_{2}}.

(149)

D.3.2
\widehat{VP}_{2}

For the second term, Eq. (
82
), we find the following active space expression:

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{\mathrm{2,active}}=

\displaystyle-(4{v}^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}i_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}-2{v}^{i_{2}i_{1}}_{j_{1}i_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}})-\sum_{\mathbf{u}}(2{v}^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}i_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{u_{2}}-{v}^{i_{2}i_{1}}_{u_{1}i_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}})\hat{E}_{u_{1}u_{2}}^{+}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{t}}(2{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}i_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}+2{v}^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}t_{2}}S^{t_{1}}_{j_{1}}-{v}^{t_{1}i}_{jt_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}-{v}^{i_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}i_{1}}S^{t_{1}}_{j_{1}})\hat{E}_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{+}-\sum_{\mathbf{t}}{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}t_{4}}S^{t_{3}}_{j_{1}}\hat{E}^{+}_{t_{1}t_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}}v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}i_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{u_{1}u_{2}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}(2v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}t_{2}}S^{t_{1}}_{u_{2}}-v^{t_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}t_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}}-v^{i_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}i_{1}}S^{t_{1}}_{u_{2}})\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{u_{1}u_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}\end{subarray}}{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}t_{4}}S^{t_{3}}_{u_{2}}\hat{e}^{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}_{\mathbf{t}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}.

(150)

D.3.3
\widehat{VP}_{3}

Next, we find the following expression for the active space version of the third term, Eq. (
87
):

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{\mathrm{3,active}}=

\displaystyle-(4v^{i_{1}j_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S_{i}^{j_{2}}-2v^{i_{1}j_{2}}_{j_{2}j}S_{i_{1}}^{j_{1}})-\sum_{\mathbf{t}}(v^{t_{1}j_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S_{t_{2}}^{j_{2}}-v^{t_{1}j_{2}}_{j_{2}j}S_{t_{2}}^{j_{1}})\hat{E}^{+}_{t_{1}t_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{u}}(2v^{i_{1}j_{1}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S_{i_{1}}^{j_{1}}+2v^{i_{1}u_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S_{i_{1}}^{u_{1}}-v^{i_{1}u_{2}}_{u_{1}j_{1}}S_{i_{1}}^{j_{1}}-v^{i_{1}j_{1}}_{ju_{1}}S_{i_{1}}^{u_{2}})\hat{E}^{+}_{u_{1}u_{2}}-\sum_{\mathbf{u}}v^{i_{1}u_{4}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S_{i_{1}}^{u_{3}}\hat{E}^{+}_{u_{1}u_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}}v^{t_{1}j_{1}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S_{t_{2}}^{j_{1}}\hat{E}^{+}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{u_{1}u_{2}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}(2v^{t_{1}u_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S_{t_{2}}^{u_{1}}-v^{t_{1}u_{2}}_{u_{1}j_{1}}S_{t_{2}}^{j_{1}}-v^{t_{1}j_{1}}_{ju_{1}}S_{t_{2}}^{u_{2}})\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{u_{1}u_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}\end{subarray}}v^{t_{1}u_{4}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S_{t_{2}}^{u_{3}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{1}}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{e}^{\sigma_{2}\sigma_{1}}_{\mathbf{u}}.

(151)

D.3.4
\widehat{VP}_{4}

Finally, we find the following active space operator for the fourth term, Eq. (
93
):

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{\mathrm{4,active}}=

\displaystyle-(8v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{2}}S_{i_{2}}^{j_{2}}-4v^{i_{1}i_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}-4v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{2}}+2v^{i_{1}i_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{2}})

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{u}}(4v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}-2v^{i_{1}i_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}+4v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{u_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{u_{1}}-2v^{i_{1}i_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{u_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{1}}

\displaystyle-2v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}j_{1}}S^{i_{2}}_{u_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}+v^{i_{1}i_{2}}_{u_{1}j_{1}}S^{i_{2}}_{u_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}-2v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}u_{1}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{2}}_{u_{2}}+v^{i_{1}i_{2}}_{j_{1}u_{1}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}})\hat{E}^{+}_{u_{1}u_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}\end{subarray}}(2v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{u_{4}}S^{i_{2}}_{u_{3}}-v^{i_{1}i_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{u_{4}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{3}})\hat{e}^{+}_{u_{1}u_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{t}}(4v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{2}}-2v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{2}}+4v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{t_{1}}_{j_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{2}}-2v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{t_{1}}_{j_{1}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{2}}

\displaystyle-2v^{t_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{2}}+v^{t_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{2}}-2v^{it_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{2}}+v^{it_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{2}})\hat{E}^{+}_{t_{1}t_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\\
\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}\end{subarray}}(2v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S_{t_{3}j_{2}}S_{t_{4}j_{2}}-v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S_{t_{3}j}S_{t_{4}j_{2}})\hat{e}^{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}_{t_{1}t_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\end{subarray}}(2v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}+2v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}}-v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}-v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}u_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}}+2v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{t_{1}}_{j_{1}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{1}}

\displaystyle-v^{t_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{1}}-v^{i_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{t_{1}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}})\hat{E}^{+}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{u_{1}u_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}(4v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{t_{1}}_{u_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{u_{1}}-2v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}j_{1}}S^{t_{1}}_{u_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{j}-2v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{ju_{2}}S^{t_{1}}_{j}S^{t_{2}}_{u_{1}}-2v^{t_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{u_{1}}-2v^{t_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{u_{1}}

\displaystyle+v^{t_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{1}}+v^{t_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}u_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}S^{t_{2}}_{u_{2}}+v^{i_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}j_{1}}S^{t_{1}}_{u_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}+v^{i_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}u_{2}}S^{t_{1}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{1}})\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{\mathbf{t}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{\mathbf{u}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}\end{subarray}}(2v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{t_{1}}_{u_{4}}S^{t_{2}}_{u_{3}}-v^{t_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{4}}S^{t_{2}}_{u_{3}}-v^{it_{1}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{u_{4}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{3}})\hat{E}^{\sigma_{2}}_{\mathbf{t}}\hat{e}^{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}_{\mathbf{u}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}\sigma_{3}\end{subarray}}v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{4}}S^{i_{2}}_{u_{3}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{1}}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{e}^{\sigma_{2}\sigma_{3}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}\sigma_{3}\end{subarray}}v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{t_{3}}_{j_{1}}S^{t_{4}}_{j_{1}}\hat{e}^{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{3}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}\end{subarray}}(2v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{t_{3}}_{u_{2}}S^{t_{4}}_{u_{1}}-v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}j_{1}}S^{t_{3}}_{u_{2}}S^{t_{4}}_{j_{1}}-v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}u_{1}}S^{t_{3}}_{j_{1}}S^{t_{4}}_{u_{2}})\hat{e}^{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}_{\mathbf{t}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{2}}_{\mathbf{u}}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}\sigma_{3}\end{subarray}}v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{t_{3}}_{u_{4}}S^{t_{4}}_{u_{3}}\hat{e}^{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{3}}_{\mathbf{t}}\hat{e}^{\sigma_{2}\sigma_{3}}_{\mathbf{u}}

(152)

Combining all four terms, the active space electrostatic-exchange operator may be written as:

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{\mathrm{active}}

\displaystyle=VP_{0,\mathrm{active}}+\widehat{VP}_{A,\mathrm{active}}+\widehat{VP}_{B,\mathrm{active}}+\widehat{VP}_{1m,\mathrm{active}}

\displaystyle+\widehat{VP}_{1\ell,\mathrm{active}}+\widehat{VP}_{2,\mathrm{active}}+\widehat{VP}_{3,\mathrm{active}}+\widehat{VP}_{4,\mathrm{active}}

(153)

where

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{A,\mathrm{active}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}\tilde{f}_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{(A)}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{t_{1}t_{2}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\\
\sigma\tau\end{subarray}}\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{t_{3}t_{4}}\,\hat{e}_{\mathbf{t}}^{\sigma\tau},

(154)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{B,\mathrm{active}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}\tilde{f}_{u_{1}u_{2}}^{(B)}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{u_{1}u_{2}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma\tau\end{subarray}}\tilde{v}^{u_{1}u_{2}}_{u_{3}u_{4}}\hat{e}_{\mathbf{u}}^{\sigma\tau},

(155)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{1m,\mathrm{active}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{u_{1}u_{2}},

(156)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{1\ell,\mathrm{active}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\end{subarray}}\tilde{v}^{t_{1}u_{2}}_{u_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{u_{1}u_{2}},

(157)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{2,\mathrm{active}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma\tau\end{subarray}}\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}t_{4}}S^{t_{3}}_{u_{2}}\,\hat{e}^{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}_{\mathbf{t}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{2}}_{\mathbf{u}},

(158)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{3,\mathrm{active}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}\end{subarray}}\tilde{v}^{t_{1}u_{4}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{u_{3}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{2}}_{\mathbf{t}}\hat{e}^{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}_{\mathbf{u}},

(159)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{4,\mathrm{active}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}\sigma_{3}\end{subarray}}\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}\tilde{p}^{t_{3}t_{4}}_{u_{3}u_{4}}\;\hat{e}^{\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}}_{\mathbf{t}}\hat{e}^{\sigma_{3}\sigma_{2}}_{\mathbf{u}},

(160)

with renormalized active space tensor coefficients defined as:

\displaystyle VP_{0,\mathrm{active}}=

\displaystyle-2v^{i_{1}j_{1}}_{j_{1}i_{1}}-(4{v}^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}i_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}-2{v}^{i_{2}i_{1}}_{j_{1}i_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}})-(4v^{i_{1}j_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{2}}-2v^{i_{1}j_{2}}_{j_{2}j}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}})

(161)

\displaystyle-(8v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{2}}-4v^{i_{1}i_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{2}}-4v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{2}}+2v^{i_{1}i_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{2}})

(162)

\displaystyle\tilde{f}_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{(A)}=

\displaystyle-v^{j_{1}t_{2}}_{t_{1}j_{1}}-(2v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}i_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}+2{v}^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}t_{2}}S^{t_{1}}_{j_{1}}-{v}^{t_{1}i}_{jt_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}-{v}^{i_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}i_{1}}S^{t_{1}}_{j_{1}})-(v^{t_{1}j_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{2}}-v^{t_{1}j_{2}}_{j_{2}j}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{1}})

(163)

\displaystyle-(4v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{2}}-2v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{2}}-2v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{t_{1}}_{j_{1}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{2}}-2v^{t_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{2}}+v^{t_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{2}}-2v^{it_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{2}}+v^{it_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{2}})

\displaystyle\tilde{f}_{u_{1}u_{2}}^{(B)}=

\displaystyle-v^{i_{1}u_{2}}_{u_{1}i_{1}}-(2{v}^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}i_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{u_{2}}-{v}^{i_{2}i_{1}}_{u_{1}i_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}})-(2v^{i_{1}j_{1}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}+2v^{i_{1}u_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{1}}-v^{i_{1}u_{2}}_{u_{1}j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}-v^{i_{1}j_{1}}_{ju_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}})

(164)

\displaystyle-(4v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}-2v^{i_{1}i_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}-2v^{i_{1}i_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{u_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{1}}-2v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}j_{1}}S^{i_{2}}_{u_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}+v^{i_{1}i_{2}}_{u_{1}j_{1}}S^{i_{2}}_{u_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}-2v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}u_{1}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{2}}_{u_{2}}+v^{i_{1}i_{2}}_{j_{1}u_{1}}S^{i_{2}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}})

\displaystyle\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{t_{3}t_{4}}=

\displaystyle-{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}t_{4}}S^{t_{3}}_{j_{1}}+v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{t_{3}}_{j_{1}}S^{t_{4}}_{j_{2}}

(165)

\displaystyle\tilde{v}^{u_{1}u_{2}}_{u_{3}u_{4}}=

\displaystyle-v^{i_{1}u_{4}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{3}}+v^{i_{1}i_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{i_{2}}_{u_{4}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{3}}

(166)

\displaystyle\tilde{v}^{t_{1}u_{2}}_{u_{1}t_{2}}=

\displaystyle-v^{t_{1}u_{2}}_{u_{1}t_{2}}-(2v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}t_{2}}S^{t_{1}}_{u_{2}}-v^{t_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}t_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}}-v^{i_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}i_{1}}S^{t_{1}}_{u_{2}})-(2v^{t_{1}u_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{u_{1}}-v^{t_{1}u_{2}}_{u_{1}j_{1}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{1}}-v^{t_{1}j_{1}}_{ju_{1}}S^{t_{2}}_{u_{2}})

(167)

\displaystyle+(4v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}j_{1}}S^{t_{1}}_{u_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{j}+4v^{t_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{u_{1}}-2v^{t_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{1}}-2v^{t_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}u_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}S^{t_{2}}_{u_{2}})

\displaystyle\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}=

\displaystyle-v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}i_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}}-v^{t_{1}j_{1}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{1}}-(2v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}-2v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}j_{1}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}-2v^{t_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{j_{1}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{1}})

(168)

\displaystyle\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}t_{4}}=

\displaystyle-(v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}t_{4}}-2v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}j_{1}}S^{t_{4}}_{j_{1}})

(169)

\displaystyle\tilde{v}^{t_{1}u_{4}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}=

\displaystyle-({v}^{t_{1}u_{4}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}-2v^{t_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{4}})

(170)

\displaystyle\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}=

\displaystyle+v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}+2v^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}\delta_{u_{3}u_{4}}/\tilde{\eta}_{B}+2v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}\delta_{t_{3}t_{4}}/\tilde{\eta}_{A}+4v^{i_{1}i_{1}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}\delta_{t_{3}t_{4}}\delta_{u_{3}u_{4}}/(\tilde{\eta}_{A}\tilde{\eta}_{B})

(171)

\displaystyle\tilde{p}^{t_{3}t_{4}}_{u_{3}u_{4}}=

\displaystyle-S^{t_{3}}_{u_{4}}S^{t_{4}}_{u_{3}}-S^{t_{3}}_{j_{1}}S^{t_{4}}_{j_{1}}\delta_{u_{3}u_{4}}/\tilde{\eta}_{B\sigma}-S^{i_{1}}_{u_{3}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{4}}\delta_{t_{3}t_{4}}/\tilde{\eta}_{A\sigma}.

(172)

Converting to chemist notation, we obtain:

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{A,\mathrm{active}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}(\tilde{f}_{t_{1}t_{2}}^{(A)}-\sum_{t}\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t}_{tt_{2}})\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{t_{1}t_{2}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\\
\sigma\tau\end{subarray}}\tilde{\nu}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{t_{3}t_{4}}\,\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{\tau}_{t_{3}t_{4}},

(173)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{B,\mathrm{active}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma\end{subarray}}(\tilde{f}_{u_{1}u_{2}}^{(B)}-\sum_{u}\tilde{v}^{u_{1}u}_{uu_{2}})\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{u_{1}u_{2}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma\tau\end{subarray}}\tilde{\nu}^{u_{1}u_{2}}_{u_{3}u_{4}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{u_{1}u_{2}}\hat{E}^{\tau}_{u_{3}u_{4}},

(174)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{1m,\mathrm{active}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\end{subarray}}\tilde{\nu}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{+}_{u_{1}u_{2}},

(175)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{1\ell,\mathrm{active}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\end{subarray}}\tilde{\nu}^{t_{1}u_{2}}_{u_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma}_{u_{1}u_{2}},

(176)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{2,\mathrm{active}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}\end{subarray}}\tilde{\nu}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}t_{4}}S^{t_{3}}_{u_{2}}\,\hat{E}^{\sigma_{1}}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{2}}_{t_{3}t_{4}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}},

(177)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{3,\mathrm{active}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}\end{subarray}}\tilde{\nu}^{t_{1}u_{4}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{u_{3}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{2}}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{1}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{2}}_{u_{3}u_{4}},

(178)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{4,\mathrm{active}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mathbf{t}\mathbf{u}\\
\sigma_{1}\sigma_{2}\sigma_{3}\end{subarray}}\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}\tilde{p}^{t_{3}t_{4}}_{u_{3}u_{4}}\;\hat{E}^{\sigma_{1}}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{2}}_{t_{3}t_{4}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{3}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}\hat{E}^{\sigma_{2}}_{u_{3}u_{4}},

(179)

where the active space tensor coefficients in the chemist notation are given by,

\displaystyle\tilde{\nu}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}

\displaystyle=\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}-\sum_{t_{3}}\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{3}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{t_{3}}_{j_{1}}S^{t_{2}}_{j_{1}}-\sum_{u_{3}}\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{3}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{3}}S^{i_{1}}_{u_{2}},

(180)

\displaystyle\tilde{\nu}^{t_{1}u_{2}}_{u_{1}t_{2}}

\displaystyle=\tilde{v}^{t_{1}u_{2}}_{u_{1}t_{2}}+\sum_{t_{3}u_{3}}\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{3}}_{u_{1}u_{3}}S^{t_{3}}_{u_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{u_{2}}-\sum_{u_{3}}\tilde{v}_{u_{1}u_{3}}^{t_{1}u_{2}}S^{t_{2}}_{u_{3}}-\sum_{t_{3}}\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{3}}_{u_{1}t_{2}}S^{t_{3}}_{u_{2}},

(181)

\displaystyle\tilde{\nu}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}t_{4}}

\displaystyle=\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}t_{4}}-\sum_{u_{3}}\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{u_{1}u_{3}}S^{t_{4}}_{u_{3}}\,,

(182)

\displaystyle\tilde{\nu}_{u_{1}u_{2}}^{t_{1}u_{4}}

\displaystyle=\tilde{v}_{u_{1}u_{2}}^{t_{1}u_{4}}-\sum_{t_{3}}\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{3}}_{u_{1}u_{2}}S^{t_{3}}_{u_{4}}\,.

(183)

The intra-monomer tensors under the chemist notation remain equivalent, i.e
\tilde{\nu}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{t_{3}t_{4}}=\tilde{v}^{t_{1}t_{2}}_{t_{3}t_{4}}
and
\tilde{\nu}^{u_{1}u_{2}}_{u_{3}u_{4}}=\tilde{v}^{u_{1}u_{2}}_{u_{3}u_{4}}
.

Appendix E
SAPT-EVE algorithm

To ensure a first order interaction energy estimate to chemical accuracy, the target accuracy of the three observables
\hat{F}=\{\hat{V},\hat{P},\widehat{VP}\}
must be optimally chosen to simultaneously satisfy the desired accuracy and minimize computational costs. That is, we must find a target precision
\varepsilon_{F}
for every observable
\hat{F}
, such that they satisfy one over all precision
\varepsilon_{\mathrm{targ}}
while minimizing the cost of the three prime phase estimation algorithms. The resource cost minimization can be formulated as the following optimization problem,

\displaystyle\min_{\varepsilon_{V},\varepsilon_{VP},\varepsilon_{P}}\left(\frac{\lambda_{V}}{\varepsilon_{V}}+\frac{\lambda_{VP}}{\varepsilon_{VP}}+\frac{\lambda_{P}}{\varepsilon_{P}}\right)

(184)

\displaystyle\text{s.t.}\quad(1+\lambda_{P})\varepsilon_{V}+\varepsilon_{VP}+\lambda_{V}\varepsilon_{P}=\varepsilon_{\mathrm{targ}}\,.

(185)

which can be solved with the Lagrange method yielding

\displaystyle\left\{\vphantom{\frac{\sum_{1}}{\sum_{1}}}\varepsilon_{V},\,\varepsilon_{V\!P},\,\varepsilon_{P}\right\}=\frac{\varepsilon_{\mathrm{targ}}}{\sqrt{(1+\lambda_{P})\lambda_{V}}+\sqrt{\lambda_{V\!P}}+\sqrt{\lambda_{V}\lambda_{P}}}\left\{\vphantom{\frac{\sum_{1}}{\sum_{1}}}\sqrt{\frac{\lambda_{V}}{1+\lambda_{P}}},\,\sqrt{\lambda_{V\!P}},\,\sqrt{\frac{\lambda_{P}}{\lambda_{V}}}\right\}\,.

(186)

This solution is only optimal if given no further information about the expected values of
\langle\hat{V}\rangle
,
\langle\hat{P}\rangle
and
\langle\widehat{VP}\rangle
. We have also upper bounded expectation values
\langle F\rangle
with their respective
\ell_{1}
norms
\lambda_{F}
in Eq. (
185
), which is a very loose bound. In practice, one will certainly be able to relax the target accuracies by bootstrapping Eq. (
185
) with low accuracy estimates
\langle F\rangle_{\text{low}}
, replacing
\lambda_{F}\mapsto\langle F\rangle_{\text{low}}
.

For every observable
\hat{F}
we have to adjust the polynomial degrees of SAPT-EVE’s inner phase estimation routines
\mathsf{iQPE_{A}}
and
\mathsf{iQPE_{B}}
according to the target errors
\varepsilon_{F}
. While we have demonstrated this procedure in Ref.
Steudtner et al. 2023
for QSP-EVE, it is not immediately clear how the discretization errors of two inner phase estimation routines would be dealt with in SAPT-EVE. Let us quickly summarize the estimation procedure behind QSP-EVE and SAPT-EVE. The iterate of Figure
2
is the product of two reflections
\mathcal{R}_{\pi}
and
\mathcal{R}_{\tau}
, each of which has a structure
\mathcal{R}_{\mathfrak{X}}=1-2\hat{\mathfrak{X}}
with some arbitrary-rank projectors
\hat{\mathfrak{X}}=\hat{\pi}
,
\hat{\tau}
. The product
\mathcal{R}_{\tau}\mathcal{R}_{\pi}
now has the eigenvalues
\exp(\pm i2\arccos w_{k})
, where
w_{k}
are the singular values of the product of projectors
\hat{\pi}\cdot\hat{\tau}
. The idea behind SAPT-EVE is now that one of the singular values is a function of
\langle\hat{F}\rangle
. In a world without discretization errors, the circuits we employ would fix the reference states exactly:

\displaystyle\mathsf{iQPE}^{\dagger}_{q}\,\mathsf{Refl}^{\phantom{\dagger}}_{q}\,\mathsf{iQPE}^{\phantom{\dagger}}_{q}\approx 1-2|Q_{q}\rangle\!\langle Q_{q}|_{\mathsf{sim}\,q,\mathsf{enc}[H_{q}]}\otimes|\boldsymbol{0}\rangle\!\langle\boldsymbol{0}|_{\mathsf{phase}\,q}\,-\dots

(187)

for
q=\mathsf{A},\mathsf{B}
labeling routines associated with the two subsystems, and
|Q_{\mathsf{A}}\rangle
,
|Q_{\mathsf{B}}\rangle
are their respective qubitization ground states. Following the state on the right-hand side of Eq. (
187
) are other terms orthogonal to the all zero state
|\boldsymbol{0}\rangle
on the
\mathsf{phase}
register of the corresponding monomer
q=\mathsf{A},\mathsf{B}
. Considering the leading order contribution of the discretization errors, we introduce the qubitization states
|\mathcal{E}_{q}\rangle
associated with the first excited states in the respective subsystem
q
. Note that
|Q_{\mathsf{A}}\rangle
and
|\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{A}}\rangle
are associated with Hamiltonian
H_{A}
, they are
generally formed with respect to different eigenenergies than
|Q_{\mathsf{B}}\rangle
and
|\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{B}}\rangle
, which are associated with
H_{B}
. For both subsystems we introduce the projectors
\varrho^{\mathsf{A}}(E)_{\mathsf{phase\,A}}
and
\varrho^{\mathsf{B}}(E)_{\mathsf{phase\,B}}
associated with either excited state
E=\mathcal{E}

or ground state
E=Q
. We find

\displaystyle\hat{\pi}=\sum_{E=Q,\mathcal{E}}\left(\bigotimes_{q=\mathsf{A},\mathsf{B}}|E_{q}\rangle\!\langle E_{q}|_{\mathsf{sim}\,q,\mathsf{enc}[H_{q}]}\otimes\varrho^{q}(E)_{\mathsf{phase}\,q}\right)\otimes|\boldsymbol{0}\rangle\!\langle\boldsymbol{0}|_{\mathsf{enc}[F]}\,,

(188)

\displaystyle\hat{\tau}=\frac{1}{2}\left(1-\mathcal{B}[\hat{F}]\right)_{\mathsf{sim\,A,\,sim\,B,\,enc}[F]}\otimes\bigotimes_{q=\mathsf{A},\mathsf{B}}|\boldsymbol{0}\rangle\!\langle\boldsymbol{0}|_{\mathsf{enc}[H_{q}]}\,.

(189)

To investigate the product
\hat{\pi}\cdot\hat{\tau}
, we consider the singular value decomposition

\displaystyle\varrho^{q}(Q)\cdot\varrho^{q}(\mathcal{E})=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\Omega^{(q)}|\xi_{Q,q,j}\rangle\!\langle\xi_{\mathcal{E},q,j}|\,

(190)

where
\Omega^{(q)}
are singular values,
|\xi_{Q,q,j}\rangle
and
|\xi_{\mathcal{E},q,j}\rangle
are singular vectors and where the sum over
j
is in the range from
1
to
r
, the rank of
\varrho^{q}(Q)\cdot\varrho^{q}(\mathcal{E})
. We will now learn the singular values
w_{k}
of
\hat{\pi}\cdot\hat{\tau}
by computing the eigenvalues
(w_{k})^{2}
of
\hat{\pi}\cdot\hat{\tau}\cdot\hat{\pi}
, which has a block-diagonal form due to the singular value decomposition in Eq. (
190
):

\displaystyle\hat{\pi}\cdot\hat{\tau}\cdot\hat{\pi}=\bigoplus_{i,j}\frac{1}{8}\left[\begin{matrix}1-F_{00}&-F_{01}\Omega^{(\mathsf{B})}_{j}&-F_{02}\Omega^{(\mathsf{A})}_{i}&-F_{03}\Omega^{(\mathsf{A})}_{i}\Omega^{(\mathsf{B})}_{j}\\[4.0pt]
-F_{10}\Omega^{(\mathsf{B})}_{j}&1-F_{11}&-F_{12}\Omega^{(\mathsf{A})}_{i}\Omega^{(\mathsf{B})}_{j}&-F_{13}\Omega^{(\mathsf{A})}_{i}\\[4.0pt]
-F_{20}\Omega^{(\mathsf{A})}_{i}&-F_{21}\Omega^{(\mathsf{A})}_{i}\Omega^{(\mathsf{B})}_{j}&1-F_{22}&-F_{23}\Omega^{(\mathsf{B})}_{j}\\[4.0pt]
-F_{30}\Omega^{(\mathsf{A})}_{i}\Omega^{(\mathsf{B})}_{j}&-F_{31}\Omega^{(\mathsf{A})}_{i}&-F_{32}\Omega^{(\mathsf{B})}_{j}&1-F_{33}\end{matrix}\right]_{i_{1}j_{1}},

(191)

where
F_{lm}
are some matrix elements of the observable
\hat{F}
, and the block matrices
[\cdots]_{i_{1}j_{1}}
denote operators with respect to the bases states

\displaystyle|Q_{\mathsf{A}}\rangle_{\mathsf{sim\,A,\,enc}[H_{A}]}\otimes|\xi_{Q,\mathsf{A},i}\rangle_{\mathsf{phase\,A}}\otimes|Q_{\mathsf{B}}\rangle_{\mathsf{sim\,B,\,enc}[H_{B}]}\otimes|\xi_{Q,\mathsf{B},j}\rangle_{\mathsf{phase\,B}}\otimes|\boldsymbol{0}\rangle_{\mathsf{enc}[F]},

(192)

\displaystyle|Q_{\mathsf{A}}\rangle_{\mathsf{sim\,A,\,enc}[H_{A}]}\otimes|\xi_{Q,\mathsf{A},i}\rangle_{\mathsf{phase\,A}}\otimes|\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{B}}\rangle_{\mathsf{sim\,B,\,enc}[H_{B}]}\otimes|\xi_{\mathcal{E},\mathsf{B},j}\rangle_{\mathsf{phase\,B}}\otimes|\boldsymbol{0}\rangle_{\mathsf{enc}[F]},

(193)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{A}}\rangle_{\mathsf{sim\,A,\,enc}[H_{A}]}\otimes|\xi_{\mathcal{E},\mathsf{A},i}\rangle_{\mathsf{phase\,A}}\otimes|Q_{\mathsf{B}}\rangle_{\mathsf{sim\,B,\,enc}[H_{B}]}\otimes|\xi_{Q,\mathsf{B},j}\rangle_{\mathsf{phase\,B}}\otimes|\boldsymbol{0}\rangle_{\mathsf{enc}[F]},

(194)

\displaystyle|\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{A}}\rangle_{\mathsf{sim\,A,\,enc}[H_{A}]}\otimes|\xi_{\mathcal{E},\mathsf{A},i}\rangle_{\mathsf{phase\,A}}\otimes|\mathcal{E}_{\mathsf{B}}\rangle_{\mathsf{sim\,B,\,enc}[H_{B}]}\otimes|\xi_{\mathcal{E},\mathsf{B},j}\rangle_{\mathsf{phase\,B}}\otimes|\boldsymbol{0}\rangle_{\mathsf{enc}[F]}\,.

(195)

For vanishing singular values,
\Omega_{j}^{(q)}=0
, we find that there is one solution of Eq. (
191
) with eigenvalue
w_{\hat{k}}^{2}=(1-F_{00})/8
. Since
F_{00}
is the matrix element associated with the ground states of both subsystems,
w_{\hat{k}}^{2}
is the solution with the eigenphase
\pm 2\arccos\sqrt{(1-\langle F\rangle)/8}
. For at least one
\Omega_{j}^{(q)}\neq 0
, the block matrices
[\cdots]_{i_{1}j_{1}}
must be diagonalized. The good solution is the one whose eigenvalue
w_{\hat{k}}^{2}
is closest to
(1-F_{00})/8
. Assuming that the contamination of this solution with contributions of excited states will increase, we replace individual
\Omega_{j}^{(\mathsf{A})}
and
\Omega_{j}^{(\mathsf{B})}
in Eq. (
191
) with one

\displaystyle\Omega=\max_{j}\max_{q=\mathsf{A,B}}\Omega_{j}^{(q)}\,.

(196)

Perturbation theory in
\Omega
informs us that the deviation of the solution from
(1-F_{00})/8
is
O(\Omega)
, only if the diagonal elements of
F_{j_{1}j_{2}}
are all equal. Assuming that the structure of
F_{i_{1}j_{1}}
is as malicious as possible, we set all
F_{j_{1}j_{2}}
to be equal. Neglecting quadratic contributions
\Omega^{2}
due to their size, we get a deviation of
w_{\hat{k}}^{2}
from
(1-F_{00})/8
that is entirely linear in
\Omega
. Note that for
\Omega=1
, we would bound
0<w_{\hat{k}}^{2}<1/4
due to
||\hat{F}||\leq 1
. This means that we can bound the error of estimated observable
F_{\text{est}}
and actual observable
\langle\hat{F}\rangle
by

\displaystyle\left|F_{\text{est}}-\langle\hat{F}\rangle\right|\leq 2\Omega\,.

(197)

We therefore need to fortify both
\mathsf{iQPE}_{q}
routines equally well against errors using quantum signal processing (QSP). Given the QSP routines, the values of
\Omega^{(\mathsf{A})}_{i}
and
\Omega^{(\mathsf{B})}_{j}
can be obtained with a numerical procedure outlined in
Steudtner et al. 2023
. The success probability of this routine, if one prepares an initial state of

\displaystyle\left(\bigotimes_{q=\mathsf{A,B}}|Q_{q}\rangle_{\mathsf{sim}\,q,\,\mathsf{enc}[H_{q}]}\otimes|\boldsymbol{0}\rangle_{\mathsf{phase}\,q}\right)\otimes|\boldsymbol{0}\rangle_{\mathsf{enc}[F]}

(198)

is between
\langle\boldsymbol{0}|\varrho^{\mathsf{A}}(Q)\otimes\varrho^{\mathsf{B}}(Q)|\boldsymbol{0}\rangle/4
and
\langle\boldsymbol{0}|\varrho^{\mathsf{A}}(Q)\otimes\varrho^{\mathsf{B}}(Q)|\boldsymbol{0}\rangle
.

Appendix F
SAPT operator encoding

In the following, we present the
sparse
and
tensor factorization
encoding methods that allow us to design a full algorithm for SAPT observable estimation. To this end, we first re-write all of the SAPT operators in terms of Majorana operators as they provide a clear and direct decomposition in terms of self-inverse operators required for block encoding.

F.1
Majorana representation

In the Majorana representation, the fermionic operators
\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p},0}
and
\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p},1}
for monomer A are defined as:

\displaystyle\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p},0}=\hat{a}_{\textsc{p}}+\hat{a}^{\dagger}_{\textsc{p}}\;\;,\;\;\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p},1}=-i(\hat{a}_{\textsc{p}}-\hat{a}^{\dagger}_{\textsc{p}}),

(199)

satisfying the properties,

\displaystyle\{\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p},i}\;,\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}^{\prime},j}\}=2\delta_{\textsc{p}\textsc{p}^{\prime}}\delta_{ij}\mathbb{1}\;\;,\;\;\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p},i}^{\dagger}=\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p},i}\;\;,\;\;\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p},i}^{2}=\mathbb{1}.

(200)

For monomer B, the Majorana operators
\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q},0}
and
\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q},1}
are defined as:

\displaystyle\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q},0}=\hat{b}_{\textsc{q}}+\hat{b}^{\dagger}_{\textsc{q}}\;\;,\;\;\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q},1}=-i(\hat{b}_{\textsc{q}}-\hat{b}^{\dagger}_{\textsc{q}}),

(201)

while also satisfying the properties,

\displaystyle\{\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q},i}\;,\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}^{\prime},j}\}=2\delta_{\textsc{q}\textsc{q}^{\prime}}\delta_{ij}\mathbb{1}\;\;,\;\;\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q},i}^{\dagger}=\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q},i}\;\;,\;\;\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q},i}^{2}=\mathbb{1}.

(202)

To find the representation of the SAPT operators in terms of the Majorana operators defined above, one may simply use the inverse relations,
\hat{a}^{\dagger}_{\textsc{p}}=\tfrac{1}{2}(\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p},0}-i\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p},1})
,
\hat{a}_{\textsc{p}}=\tfrac{1}{2}(\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p},0}+i\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p},1})
and
\hat{b}^{\dagger}_{\textsc{q}}=\tfrac{1}{2}(\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q},0}-i\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q},1})
,
\hat{b}_{\textsc{q}}=\tfrac{1}{2}(\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q},0}+i\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q},1})
. In the following, we provide the final result in both the full space and active space pictures after simplification. Throughout this section, we will use the symmetrized SAPT operators defined in Eqs. (
110
)-(
112
).

F.1.1
Full space picture

The all-electron (full space) electrostatic and exchange operators in the Majorana representation are defined as:

\displaystyle\hat{V}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{4}\sum_{\textsc{p}\textsc{q}}v^{\textsc{p}\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}\textsc{q}}+\tfrac{i}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}\end{subarray}}f^{(A)}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{2},1}+\tfrac{i}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\end{subarray}}f^{(B)}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{2},1}

(203)

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}\end{subarray}}\text{sym}(v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}})\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{2},1}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{2},1},

\displaystyle\hat{P}

\displaystyle=-\tfrac{1}{4}\sum_{\textsc{p}\textsc{q}}S^{\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}}S^{\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}}-\tfrac{i}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}\\
\textsc{q}\end{subarray}}p^{(A)}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{2},1}-\tfrac{i}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\\
\textsc{p}\end{subarray}}p^{(B)}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{2},1}

(204)

\displaystyle+\tfrac{1}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}\end{subarray}}\text{sym}(S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}})\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{2},1}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{2},1},

where we have defined
f^{(A)}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}=\sum_{\textsc{q}}v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}\textsc{q}}
,
f^{(B)}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}=\sum_{\textsc{p}}v^{\textsc{p}\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}
and
p^{(A)}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}=\sum_{\textsc{q}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}}
,
p^{(B)}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}=\sum_{\textsc{p}}S^{\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}
, as the single-body coefficients of the electrostatic and exchange operators in the Majorana representation. The electrostatic-exchange operator is written as:

\widehat{VP}^{(m)}_{\mathrm{s}}=\widehat{VP}_{A}^{(m)}+\widehat{VP}_{B}^{(m)}+\widehat{VP}_{1\mathrm{m}}^{(m)}+\widehat{VP}_{1\ell}^{(m)}+\widehat{VP}_{2}^{(m)}+\widehat{VP}_{3}^{(m)}+\widehat{VP}_{4}^{(m)},

(205)

where

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{A}^{(m)}

\displaystyle=-\tfrac{i}{4}\sum_{\textbf{{p}}}\text{sym}(\kappa_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}^{(A)})\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{2},1}+\tfrac{1}{8}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{{p}}\end{subarray}}\text{sym}(\Lambda^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}})\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{2},1}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{3},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{4},1},

(206)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{B}^{(m)}

\displaystyle=-\tfrac{i}{4}\sum_{\textbf{{q}}}\text{sym}(\kappa_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}^{(B)})\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{2},1}+\tfrac{1}{8}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{{q}}\end{subarray}}\text{sym}(\Lambda^{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}})\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{2},1}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{3},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{4},1},

(207)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{1\mathrm{m}}^{(m)}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{8}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{{p}},\textbf{{q}}\end{subarray}}\text{sym}(\Lambda^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}})\;\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{2},1}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{2},1},

(208)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{1\ell}^{(m)}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{{p}},\textbf{{q}}\end{subarray}}\text{sym}(\Lambda^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}})\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{2},1}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{2},1},

(209)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{2}^{(m)}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{i}{8}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{{p}},\textbf{{q}}\end{subarray}}\text{sym}(\Lambda^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}})\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{2},1}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{3},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{4},1}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{2},1},

(210)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{3}^{(m)}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{i}{8}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{{p}},\textbf{{q}}\end{subarray}}\text{sym}(\Lambda^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{q}_{3}}_{\textsc{p}_{2}})\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{2},1}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{2},1}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{3},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{4},1},

(211)

\displaystyle\widehat{VP}_{4}^{(m)}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{2}(\hat{V}^{\prime}\hat{P}^{\prime}+\hat{P}^{\prime}\hat{V}^{\prime}).

(212)

Here, we have defined the modified electrostatic and exchange operators
\hat{V}^{\prime}
and
\hat{P}^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\hat{V}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=-\tfrac{1}{4}\sum_{\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}}v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{2},1}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{2},1},

(213)

\displaystyle\hat{P}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=-\tfrac{i}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}\end{subarray}}p^{(A)}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{2},1}-\tfrac{i}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}\end{subarray}}p^{(B)}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{2},1}+\tfrac{1}{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textbf{{p}}\textbf{{q}}\\
\end{subarray}}\text{sym}(S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}})\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{p}_{2},1}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{1},0}\hat{\gamma}_{\textsc{q}_{2},1},

(214)

in order to reduce the
\ell_{1}
norm contribution of the total electrostatic-exchange operator. The renormalized tensor coefficients in the Majorana representation are given by,

\displaystyle{VP}_{0}^{(m)}

\displaystyle=-\tfrac{1}{4}\sum_{\textsc{p}\textsc{q}}\text{sym}(\bar{\nu}^{\textsc{p}\textsc{q}}_{\textsc{q}\textsc{p}})-\tfrac{1}{8}\sum_{\textsc{p}\textsc{q}\textsc{q}^{\prime}}\text{sym}(\nu^{\textsc{p}\textsc{q}^{\prime}}_{\textsc{q}\textsc{q}}S^{\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}^{\prime}})-\tfrac{1}{8}\sum_{\textsc{p}\textsc{p}^{\prime}\textsc{q}}\text{sym}(\nu^{\textsc{p}\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}\textsc{p}^{\prime}}S^{\textsc{p}^{\prime}}_{\textsc{q}})-\tfrac{1}{16}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textsc{p}\textsc{p}^{\prime}\\
\textsc{q}\textsc{q}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\text{sym}(v^{\textsc{p}\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}\textsc{q}}S^{\textsc{p}^{\prime}}_{\textsc{q}^{\prime}}S^{\textsc{p}^{\prime}}_{\textsc{q}^{\prime}}),

(215)

\displaystyle\kappa^{(A)}_{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\textsc{q}}\bar{\nu}^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}}_{\textsc{q}\textsc{p}_{2}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\textsc{q}\textsc{q}^{\prime}}\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{q}^{\prime}}_{\textsc{q}\textsc{q}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}^{\prime}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\textsc{p}\textsc{q}}\nu^{\textsc{p}\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}\textsc{p}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\textsc{p}\textsc{q}}\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}\textsc{p}}S^{\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}}+\tfrac{1}{4}\sum_{\textsc{p}\textsc{q}\textsc{q}^{\prime}}v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}\textsc{q}}S^{\textsc{p}^{\prime}}_{\textsc{q}^{\prime}}S^{\textsc{p}^{\prime}}_{\textsc{q}^{\prime}}+\tfrac{1}{4}\sum_{\textsc{p}\textsc{q}\textsc{q}^{\prime}}v^{\textsc{p}\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}\textsc{q}}S^{\textsc{p}_{1}}_{\textsc{q}^{\prime}}S^{\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}^{\prime}},

(216)

\displaystyle\kappa^{(B)}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\textsc{p}}\bar{\nu}^{\textsc{p}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\textsc{p}\textsc{p}^{\prime}}\nu^{\textsc{p}\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{p}^{\prime}}S^{\textsc{p}^{\prime}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\textsc{p}\textsc{q}}\nu^{\textsc{p}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}\textsc{q}}S^{\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\textsc{p}\textsc{q}}\nu^{\textsc{p}\textsc{q}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}}+\tfrac{1}{4}\sum_{\textsc{p}\textsc{p}^{\prime}\textsc{q}}v^{\textsc{p}\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}^{\prime}}_{\textsc{q}}S^{\textsc{p}^{\prime}}_{\textsc{q}}+\tfrac{1}{4}\sum_{\textsc{p}\textsc{p}^{\prime}\textsc{q}}v^{\textsc{p}\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}\textsc{q}}S^{\textsc{p}^{\prime}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}}S^{\textsc{p}^{\prime}}_{\textsc{q}_{2}},

(217)

\displaystyle\Lambda^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{p}_{3}\textsc{p}_{4}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\textsc{q}}\nu^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}\textsc{p}_{4}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\textsc{q}\textsc{q}^{\prime}}v^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}\textsc{q}}S^{\textsc{p}_{3}}_{\textsc{q}^{\prime}}S^{\textsc{p}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}^{\prime}},

(218)

\displaystyle\Lambda^{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}\textsc{q}_{4}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\textsc{p}}\nu^{\textsc{p}\textsc{q}_{4}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{\textsc{p}\textsc{p}^{\prime}}v^{\textsc{p}\textsc{p}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}S^{\textsc{p}^{\prime}}_{\textsc{q}_{3}}S^{\textsc{p}^{\prime}}_{\textsc{q}_{4}},

(219)

\displaystyle\Lambda^{\textsc{p}_{1}\textsc{p}_{2}}_{\textsc{q}_{1}\textsc{q}_{2}}
